Detailed incremental q computations:

Let n, h, q array length L=2n-1 corresponding to indices m=0..L-1, with k=m-(n-1).

Formula:
For k>=0 (m=n-1+k): q[m] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h[i] * (1 - h[i+k]). Let nv=n-k.
For k<0 (kk=-k, m=n-1-kk): q[m] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-kk} h[i+kk] * (1 - h[i]). Equivalent.

We can compute q initially by np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')? Need ensure orientation. Let's test with prefix h=[1,1,0,0]. correlate? If positive lag k=1 count 1, index 3? n=4, n-1+k=3. If np.output[3] for a=h,b=1-h? Need guess. We can implement `compute_q` directly to match grad formula, independent of np.

Function compute_q(h,n):
q = np.empty(2n-1)
for k in 0..n-1:
  nv=n-k
  q[n-1+k] = np.dot(h[:nv], 1-h[k:])
  if k>0: q[n-1-k] = np.dot(h[k:], 1-h[:nv])  # cumulative O(n^2) dot each length total O(n^2) but Python loops n=600, total 180k ops, okay but vectorized dot in loop maybe 600* average length 300 =180k, fine.
Could use FFT for speed but direct okay. For many reevaluations, direct loop too slow if repeated 10k. But for initial and final okay. For incremental after single pair, update only affected q entries; for full global recompute after batch maybe.

Derivative / coefficients for variable index a:
For each lag k>=0: if a < nv (i=a) then ∂/∂h_a = 1 - h[a+k]; if a >= k (i+k=a => i=a-k) then ∂/∂h_a = -h[a-k]. Actually q = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+k}); derivative wrt h_a: term where i=a: (1-h_{a+k}) if a≤n-1-k; term where i+k=a: -h_{a-k} if a≥k. If a equals both? possible if a = a+k? no k>0. Sum.
For k<0 (kk=-k): q = ∑ h_{i+kk}(1-h_i). Derivative wrt h_a: term where i+kk=a (i=a-kk≥0): (1-h_{a-kk}); term where i=a: -h_{a+kk} if a+kk≤n-1-kk? Actually for i from0..n-kk-1. If i=a, need a≤n-kk-1 => a+kk≤n-1. So derivative = (if a≥kk: 1-h[a-kk]) + (if a+kk < n: -h[a+kk]). For positive k formula gave for k negative? Derive unified: For any lag m, pairs (i, i+k) with k signed; q_k = ∑ h_{i+?} (1 - h_{?})? For positive k: q_k = ∑ h_i (1-h_{i+k}); derivative wrt h_a: (1-h_{a+k}) if a in first index, plus -h_{a-k} if a in second. For negative kk=q: q_-kk = ∑ h_{i+kk} (1-h_i); derivative: (1-h_{a-kk}) if a in first (shifted) index, plus -h_{a+kk} if a in second (original). So same: derivative for signed lag k: if a+k in [0,n): + (1-h[a+k]); if a-k in [0,n): -h[a-k]? Wait for positive k, second index is a-k (i=a-k, term -h_a when original h_{a-k}), yes. For negative k=-kk, a+k = a-kk in [0,n): + (1-h[a-kk]) (shifted as first), second a-k=a+kk: -h[a+kk]. Good. Unified derivative wrt h_a of q_k for signed k (where q_k = ∫ h(x)(1-h(x+k))? But for negative we used h(x+k)(1-h(x)), which equals q_-kk = ∑ h_{i+kk}(1-h_i) = ∫ h(x-kk?)? Is derivative formula unified if q_k = ∑ h_i (1-h_{i+k}) for all k (with overlap i such that both in domain)? For k=-kk, i+k=i-kk, need i≥kk, sum i=kk..n-1 h_i(1-h_{i-kk}). That is ∑ h_{i}(1-h_{i-kk}) = ∑_{j=0}^{n-kk-1} h_{j+kk}(1-h_j). Same as above. Derivative unified: q_k = ∑_{i in I_k} h_i (1-h_{i+k}), I_k={i: 0≤i<n, 0≤i+k<n}. So for positive k, I=0..n-1-k; negative kk, I=kk..n-1. Derivative wrt h_a: if a∈I: (1-h_{a+k}) (if a+k valid) plus if a-k∈I (then term h_{a-k}(1-h_a)): -h_{a-k}. So unified:
d_k(a) = (1 - h[a+k]) if 0≤a+k<n (since if a∈I, a+k valid) else 0 + (-h[a-k]) if 0≤a-k<n else 0.
Neat. This matches parent? For positive index, a=k? parent grad[0:nv]+=1-h[k:n], a in first, a+k valid; grad[k:n]+=-h[0:nv], a in second, a-k valid. For negative index (index n-1-kk), parent grad[kk:n]+=1-h[0:nv]; these are a with a+(-kk)=a-kk valid; grad[0:nv]+=-h[kk:n]; a-k=a+kk valid. Yes.

So for signed lag k, derivative coefficient for variable a is 1 - h[a+k] (if a+k valid) - h[a-k] (if a-k valid) with signs? Actually plus (-h[a-k]). Good.

Implement active weighted objective:
```
def active_obj(x,n,active,weights,mu):
    val = 0
    h=x
    for idx,wt in zip(active,weights):
       k=idx-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          val += wt * np.dot(h[:nv], 1-h[k:])
       else:
          kk=-k; nv=n-kk
          val += wt * np.dot(h[kk:n],1-h[:nv])
    val = val*dx + mu*(sum-target)^2
```
Gradient similar preallocate grad zeros, for each lag add. Use weights. This is okay for T up to 50; 50*600=30k per eval/grad; 1000 evals=30M? Actually grad with NumPy slice additions T, each C length avg n, plus Python loop T; okay.

Let's detail `active_gradient(h,n,active,weights)` using unified convention with slice per lag:
```
def grad_lag_for_weights(h,n,active,weights):
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  for idx,wt in zip(active,weights):
    k=idx-(n-1)
    if wt==0: continue
    if k>=0:
       nv=n-k
       if wt !=1: w=wt
       grad[:nv] += w*(1-h[k:])
       grad[k:] += w*(-h[:nv])
    else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk
       grad[kk:] += w*(1-h[:nv])
       grad[:nv] += w*(-h[kk:])
  return grad
```
Check for k=0: grad[:n]+=w*(1-h); grad[:n]+=w*(-h) => w*(1-2h). correct.

For active weights, choose top T lags by q. But if top lags not distinct values, uniform. To smooth, use softmax over top with alpha: weights = exp(alpha*(q_active - max_active))/sum. For pair gradient, maybe uniform active enough. For line search, use `z` from h.

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if diff>0: room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot>0: h += diff/tot*room
  else: tot=h.sum(); h += diff/tot*h
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return h
```

Direct compute_q_unified with loops and dot: 
```
def compute_q(h,n):
  L=2*n-1; q=np.empty(L)
  omh=1-h
  q[n-1]=np.dot(h,1-h)
  for k in range(1,n):
    nv=n-k
    q[n-1+k]=np.dot(h[:nv], omh[k:])
    q[n-1-k]=np.dot(h[k:], omh[:nv])
  return q
```
This is O(n^2 /2) vector dot; for n=1200 ~720k, okay. For many times maybe okay. Could use for full c: `max(q)*dx`.

`make_z`:
```
def make_pair_z(h,n,i,j):
    L=2*n-1
    z=np.zeros(L)
    omh=1-h
    mhr=-h[::-1]
    s1=n-1-i
    s2=n-1-j
    z[s1:s1+n] += omh
    z[s2:s2+n] -= omh
    z[i:i+n] += mhr
    z[j:j+n] -= mhr
    return z
```

```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,initial_scale=1.0,max_sweeps_per_scale=10,T=10,B=14):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  q=compute_q(h,n); dx=2/n; best_c=q.max()*dx; best_h=h.copy(); best_q=q.copy()
  center=n-1
  scales=[1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
  for scale in scales:
    if time>deadline: break
    improved_any=False
    for sweep in range(20):
       if time > deadline - 5: break
       h=project? maybe not each sweep. q recompute at sweep start to ensure.
       q=compute_q(h,n); cur_c=q.max()*dx
       if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14: best...
       active,weights=select_top(q,T)
       g=grad_active(h,n,active,weights)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       # low: first B smallest; high: last B largest
       low=order[:B].tolist(); high=order[-B:][::-1].tolist()
       improved_sweep=False
       # maybe random pairs: generate R=20 pairs
       random_pairs=[] if use? maybe 50
       for i in low:
         for j in high:
           if time>deadline: break
           if i==j: continue
           Ld=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0); Ud=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
           if Ud - Ld < 1e-14: continue
           z=make_pair_z(h,n,i,j)
           pref = 1.0 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1.0
           deltas=make_deltas(Ld,Ud,pref,scale)
           if not deltas: continue
           delta=choose_delta(q,z,center,n-1+(j-i),n-1+(i-j),deltas)
           if delta is not None:
              h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
              d2=delta*delta
              q += delta*z; q[c1]+=d2; q[c2]+=d2; q[center]-=2*d2
              cur_c=max(q)*dx
              if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14: best...
              improved_sweep=True
       if not improved_sweep: break
       if time>deadline: break
    # after scale no improvements, continue smaller
  # final recompute and return
```

Now, select_best_delta: Use base = q + deltas[:,None]*z. But this ignores center -2 and cross; we can add. However if `deltas` length large, `base` memory. Implement robust:
```
def choose_delta(q,z,center,c1,c2,deltas):
   d=np.asarray(deltas,float)
   if len==0: return None
   d2=d*d
   tmp=q + d[:,None]*z[None,:]
   tmp[:,c1] += d2
   tmp[:,c2] += d2
   tmp[:,center] -= 2*d2
   vals=tmp.max(axis=1)
   idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
   # verify by recomputing exact? vals counts, need compare to best? We pass best_counts? Use counts to avoid dx.
   return float(d[idx]), float(vals[idx])
```

```
def select_active(q,n,T,beta=10):
   idx=np.argpartition(q, -T)[-T:]
   # sort by q desc for weights
   idx=idx[np.argsort(q[idx])[::-1]]
   vals=q[idx]
   rng_val=vals[0]-vals[-1]
   if rng_val<1e-9: w=np.ones(T)/T
   else:
      w=np.exp(beta*(vals-vals[0])) # values <=1, maybe underflow; normalize
      if w.sum()<1: w=np.ones(T)/T
      else: w/=w.sum()
   return idx,w
```

def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,mu=0):

```
def weighted_grad(x,n,active,weights,mu):
  grad=np.zeros(n,float)
  sumdiff=x.sum()-0.5*n
  if mu*sumdiff !=0: grad += 2*mu*sumdiff
  for idx,wt in zip(active,weights):
    if wt <=1e-14: continue
    k=idx-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
      nv=n-k
      if wt==1: grad[:nv] += 1-x[k:]; grad[k:] -= x[:nv]
      else: grad[:nv] += wt*(1-x[k:]); grad[k:] -= wt*x[:nv]
    else: ...
  return grad
```
Penalty gradient added to all, but if also adding inside loops? do once.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=0.5*n
  for _ in range(50):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      # if diff>tot? scale? distribute all room; then residual
      h += (diff/tot)*room
    else:
      tot=h.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h += (diff/tot)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # final adjust one coordinate for tiny diff
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     if diff>0:
       idx=np.argmax(1-h)
       h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
     else:
       idx=np.argmax(h)
       h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
     # may still not exact; repeat up to
  return h
```
If diff > room sum, `h += diff/tot*room` makes all to1 but sum too high? If target<=n always and after clip target achievable, diff≤room sum. Good.

`select_active`:
```
def select_active(q,n,T,beta=8.0):
  L=len(q); T=min(T,L)
  idx=np.argpartition(q, -T)[-T:]
  vals=q[idx]
  order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
  idx=idx[order]; vals=vals[order]
  rngv=vals[0]-vals[-1]
  if rngv < 1e-8:
    w=np.full(T,1.0/T)
  else:
    w=np.exp(-beta*(vals-vals[-1]) / max(rngv,1e-12))? This would weight low more; better weight high.
```
We want weights on high lags. Let `s=beta*(vals - vals[0])` (<=0). `w=exp(s)`. Then max lag weight 1. Normalize. If beta=8 and range small, nearly uniform; if range large, top gets more.
```
    s=beta*(vals-vals[0])
    w=np.exp(s)
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw<1e-300: w=np.full(T,1/T)
    else: w/=sw
```

def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,scales=None):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); q=compute_q(h,n); dx=2/n
  cur_count=float(np.max(q)); best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy(); center=n-1
  B=...
  scales = ...
  for scale in scales:
    if time>deadline: break
    improved_over_scale=True
    while improved_over_scale and time<deadline-2:
       # optional recompute q (already)
       if time>deadline: break
       q=compute_q(h,n); cur_count=np.max(q); if cur_count<best_count: best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
       # select active
       active,weights=select_active(q,n,T=12)
       g=grad_active(h,n,active,weights)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       low=order[:B]; high=order[-B:][::-1]
       improved=False
       # pair loop
       for i in low:
         for j in high:
           ...
           if accepted: improved=True; cur_count=... maybe < best; if best update? avoid copy each
       # random pairs if time
       if improved:
          # after sweep, project and recompute?
          if accepted_count > 20: h=project_feasible... q=compute_q...
       if not improved:
          improved_over_scale=False
          # maybe one more scale
    if time>deadline: break
  h=project_feasible(h,n); q=compute_q; cur=...
  if cur<best_count: best_h=h.copy(); best_count=cur
  return best_h, best_count*dx
```

`choose_delta` code:
```
def choose_delta(q,z,center,c1,c2,deltas,target_count,tol=1e-11):
  d=np.asarray(deltas,float)
  if d.size==0: return None
  d2=d*d
  tmp=q + d[:,None]*z[None,:]
  tmp[:,c1]+=d2; tmp[:,c2]+=d2; tmp[:,center]-=2*d2
  vals=tmp.max(axis=1)
  imin=int(np.argmin(vals))
  if vals[imin] < target_count - tol:
     return float(d[imin]), float(vals[imin])
  return None
```
No dx. target_count current max. If target_count maybe slightly lower than q.max due rounding? recompute.

def binary_anneal(h0,n,deadline,rng):
  h=binarize(h0,n)  # ensure
  q=compute_q(h,n); cur_count=np.max(q); best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy(); center=n-1
  ones=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(h)]
  zeros=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(1-h)]
  one_pos={int(i):p for p,i in enumerate(ones)} # dict top-level? okay
  zero_pos=...
  best_temp=...
  for temp in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.0):
    if time>deadline: break
    nonaccept=0
    max_iters = 200000? need time. We'll loop while time<deadline-2 and nonaccept<500.
    while time<deadline-2:
       if not ones or not zeros: break
       i = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
       j = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
       # move i zero to one, j to zero
       z=make_pair_z(h,n,i,j) # i zero, j one, δ=1
       tmp=q+z  # alloc
       tmp[c1]+=1; tmp[c2]+=1; tmp[center]-=2
       new_count=tmp.max()
       if new_count <= cur_count + temp and new_count < best_count? accept if <=? If new_count > cur_count+temp reject.
       if accept:
          h[i]=1; h[j]=0; q=tmp.copy()? We can set q = tmp (already new array) to avoid copy. Good.
          cur_count = new_count
          if cur_count < best_count: best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
          # update lists
          # remove i from zeros
          p=zero_pos[i]; last=zzeros[-1]; zeros[p]=last; zero_pos[last]=p; zeros.pop(); del zero_pos[i]
          # add i to ones
          pos=len(ones); ones.append(i); one_pos[i]=pos
          # remove j from ones
          p=one_pos[j]; last=ones[-1]; ones[p]=last; one_pos[last]=p; ones.pop(); del one_pos[j]
          # add j to zeros
          pos=len(zeros); zeros.append(j); zero_pos[j]=pos
          nonaccept=0
       else:
          nonaccept +=1
          if nonaccept > 20000: break  # saturate
    # next temp
  h=project? binary sum preserved.
  return best_h, best_count*dx
```

`compute_q`: ensure h float contiguous.

```
def compute_q(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    q=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    omh=1.0-h
    q[n-1]=np.dot(h, omh)
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       q[n-1+k]=np.dot(h[:nv], omh[k:])
       q[n-1-k]=np.dot(h[k:], omh[:nv])
    return q
```
This loops k=1..n-1; for n=1? if n=1 q[0]=0. okay.

`c5_from_q(q,n)`: `float(np.max(q)) * 2.0/n`.

`add_lag_grad(h,n,grad,idx,wt)`.

```
def weighted_obj(x,n,active,weights,mu=0.0):
    dx=2.0/n
    val=0.0
    for idx,wt in zip(active,weights):
       if wt<=0: continue
       k=idx-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
         nv=n-k; val += wt*np.dot(x[:nv], 1.0-x[k:])
       else: ...
    diff=x.sum()-0.5*n
    return dx*val + mu*diff*diff
```
Could optimize `1.0-x` create each loop. Fine.

```
def weighted_grad(x,n,active,weights,mu=0.0):
  dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n,float)
  for idx,wt...
     add_lag_grad(x,n,grad,idx,wt)
  grad *= dx
  diff=x.sum()-0.5*n
  if mu and diff!=0: grad += 2*mu*diff
  return grad
```
`add_lag_grad` uses wt.

`active_lbfgs_phase` use `minimize`; bounds as list. `options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50}`. For n large, gtol 1e-10.

```
def active_lbfgs_phase(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    q=compute_q(h,n); best_c=c5_from_q(q,n); best_h=h.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    if n<=400: base_iter=70
    elif n<=800: base_iter=40
    else: base_iter=25
    configs=[(6,10.0,base_iter),(12,100.0,int(base_iter*0.8)),(24,100.0,int(base_iter*0.6))]
    for T,mu,mx in configs:
       for cycle in range(2):
          if time.time()>deadline-2: break
          q=compute_q(h,n)
          active,weights=select_active(q,n,T)
          try:
             res=minimize(weighted_obj,h,args=(n,active,weights,mu),jac=weighted_grad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50})
          except Exception: continue
          h_new=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,float),n)
          c= c5_exact(h_new,n) # to be safe? Exact np correlate O(n^2) okay per res.
          if c < best_c - 1e-14:
             best_c=c; best_h=h_new.copy(); h=h_new
          # else maybe h = best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```
Using exact c after each res costs np.correlate O(n^2) 300/600, fine. For n=600, maybe 10 res -> 3.6M ops, okay.

SLSQP functions:
```
def _slsqp_obj(x,n,active,weights):
   dx=2.0/n; val=0; ...; return dx*val
def _slsqp_global_obj? weighted_obj with mu=0 but no penalty.
def _slsqp_grad(x,n,active,weights):
   dx=2.0/n; grad zeros; add; return grad*dx
def _slsqp_eq(x,n): return x.sum()-0.5*n
def _slsqp_jac(x,n): return np.ones(len(x))
```

Pair functions:

```
def make_pair_z(h,n,i,j):
    L=2*n-1
    z=np.zeros(L,float)
    omh=1.0-h
    mhr=-h[::-1]
    s1=n-1-i; s2=n-1-j
    z[s1:s1+n] += omh
    z[s2:s2+n] -= omh
    z[i:i+n] += mhr
    z[j:j+n] -= mhr
    return z
```

`make_deltas`:
```
def make_deltas(Ld,Ud,pref,scale):
   span=Ud-Ld
   if span <= 1e-14: return []
   vals=[]; maxc=24
   for s in BASE_STEPS:
      d=pref*s*scale
      if d > Ld and d < Ud: vals.append(d)
      if len(vals) >= maxc: break
      d=-pref*s*scale
      if d > Ld and d < Ud and len(vals) < maxc: vals.append(d)
   # relative
   mid=0.5*(Ld+Ud)
   for f in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7):
      for sgn in (pref,-pref):
         if len(vals)>=maxc: break
         d=mid + sgn*f*0.5*span
         if d>Ld and d<Ud: vals.append(d)
   # unique
   uniq=[]; seen=set()
   for v in vals:
      if v<=Ld or v>=Ud: continue
      key=round(v,14)
      if key not in seen:
         seen.add(key); uniq.append(float(v))
   return uniq
```
But `BASE_STEPS` multiplied by scale; if scale small, steps smaller. At scale 1, first step 0.05. The loop breaks when len>=maxc; if interval small, many steps invalid and loop goes through all. Good.

`choose_delta` as above.

`pair_polish` detailed:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=0):
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
  q=compute_q(h,n); dx=2.0/n
  cur_count=float(np.max(q)); best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy(); center=n-1
  B=12 if n<=400 else 8 if n<=800 else 6
  T=12 if n<=400 else 16
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  scales=PAIR_SCALES
  tol=1e-9
  for scale in scales:
    if time.time() > deadline - 1: break
    improved_in_scale=True
    while improved_in_scale and time.time() < deadline - 1:
       # reset if current worse than best
       q=compute_q(h,n)
       cur_count=float(np.max(q))
       if cur_count < best_count - tol:
          best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
       elif cur_count > best_count + tol:
          h=best_h.copy(); q=compute_q(h,n); cur_count=best_count
       # select active and grad
       active,weights=select_active(q,n,T)
       g=grad_active(h,n,active,weights)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       low=order[:B]
       high=order[-B:][::-1]
       improved=False
       moves=0
       # guided pairs
       for ii in range(B):
         i=int(low[ii])
         for jj in range(B):
           j=int(high[jj])
           if i==j: continue
           hi=float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
           Ld=max(-hi, hj-1.0); Ud=min(1.0-hi, hj)
           if Ud-Ld < 1e-13: continue
           z=make_pair_z(h,n,i,j)
           pref = 1.0 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1.0
           deltas=make_deltas(Ld,Ud,pref,scale)
           if not deltas: continue
           res=choose_delta(q,z,center,center+(j-i),center+(i-j),deltas,cur_count,tol)
           if res is not None:
              delta, new_count = res
              h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
              d2=delta*delta
              q += delta*z
              q[center+(j-i)] += d2
              q[center+(i-j)] += d2
              q[center] -= 2.0*d2
              # clip tiny? if h out tiny, no
              cur_count=new_count
              moves +=1
              if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                 best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
              improved=True
              if moves >= 60:
                 break
         if moves >=60: break
       # random pairs
       if time.time() < deadline - 1:
          rand_count = min(20, max(5, B//2))
          for _ in range(rand_count):
             if time.time() > deadline: break
             i=int(rng.randrange(n)); j=int(rng.randrange(n))
             if i==j: continue
             ... same with pref maybe rng
       if not improved:
          improved_in_scale=False
       else:
          if moves > 50 or True:
             h=project_feasible(h,n)
             q=compute_q(h,n)
             cur_count=float(np.max(q))
             if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
             elif cur_count > best_count + tol:
                h=best_h.copy(); q=compute_q(h,n); cur_count=best_count
  h=project_feasible(h,n); q=compute_q(h,n); cur=...
  if cur<best_count: best_h=h.copy(); best_count=cur
  return best_h, best_count*dx
```
Need avoid `b` loops break out elegantly. Use flags.

The inner while repeats a full sweep until no improvement. But after each sweep we project/recompute; if project slightly worsens, revert to best. However if project worsens relative to best, we set h=best and then the while may find same improvements? possible loop. But project should not worsen much. To prevent, if projection makes cur_count > best_count + 10*tol, revert and break? Could set improved_in_scale=False? But then maybe no further. Let's implement:
```
if cur_count <= best_count + 1e-7: continue
else: h=best_h.copy(); ...; improved_in_scale=False; break
```
Actually projection is should lower; if not, stops.

But note: `best_h` is a copy at best. If current has made progress but projection worsens (unlikely), we rollback to best and stop scale. Fine.

The pair loop modifies q incrementally; after many moves, q exact. But `choose_delta` uses `cur_count` target. If a later candidate doesn't improve current but would improve best from a different current? no.

Potential issue: `choose_delta` uses `target_count` cur_count. It only accepts if candidate max < target_count. The minimum candidate could be greater than target but there might be a move that first increases then later decreases; pair line search only descent. That's fine for local.

def binary_anneal(h0,n,deadline,seed=0):
   h=binarize(h0,n)
   q=compute_q(h,n); dx=2.0/n; cur_count=np.max(q); best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy(); center=n-1
   ones=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(h)]
   zeros=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(1.0-h)]
   one_pos={int(i):p for p,i in enumerate(ones)}
   zero_pos={int(i):p for p,i in enumerate(zeros)}
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   temps=(2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625)
   for temp in temps:
      no_acc=0
      max_no_acc = 20000 if temp>=1 else 5000
      while time.time() < deadline - 1 and no_acc < max_no_acc:
         if not ones or not zeros: break
         i=zero?; j=one?
         z=make_pair_z(h,n,i,j)
         c1=center+(j-i); c2=center+(i-j)
         tmp=q+z
         tmp[c1]+=1.0; tmp[c2]+=1.0; tmp[center]-=2.0
         new_count=float(np.max(tmp))
         if new_count <= cur_count + temp:
            h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0; q=tmp; cur_count=new_count
            if cur_count < best_count:
               best_count=cur_count; best_h=h.copy()
            # update lists
            # remove i from zeros
            p=zero_pos[i]; last=zeros[-1]; zeros[p]=last; zero_pos[last]=p; zeros.pop(); del zero_pos[i]
            # add i to ones
            p=len(ones); ones.append(i); one_pos[i]=p
            # remove j from ones
            p=one_pos[j]; last=ones[-1]; ones[p]=last; one_pos[last]=p; ones.pop(); del one_pos[j]
            # add j to zeros
            p=len(zeros); zeros.append(j); zero_pos[j]=p
            no_acc=0
         else:
            no_acc +=1
            # optional random restart if temp small? no
      # after temperature, maybe local hill temp=1e-6? included
   h=best_h.copy(); q=compute_q(h,n); return best_h,float(np.max(q))*dx

Potential syntax issue: In `project_feasible`, final residual adjustment if diff positive and idx chosen but h[i]+diff >1; we clip but sum not exact. Could loop. Implement simple:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=0.5*n
   for _ in range(80):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
         if tot<1e-15: break
         add=diff/tot*room
         h=np.clip(h+add,0,1)
      else:
         tot=h.sum()
         if tot<1e-15: break
         h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*h,0,1)
   # micro-adjust
   diff=target-h.sum()
   for _ in range(10):
      if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      if diff>0:
         idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
         delta=min(diff, max(0.0,1.0-h[idx]))
         if delta<=0: break
         h[idx]+=delta; diff-=delta; target? Actually h.sum changed, recompute
      else:
         idx=int(np.argmax(h))
         delta=min(-diff, h[idx])
         if delta<=0: break
         h[idx]-=delta
   return h
```
But after micro adjust recompute diff? Use while with `diff=target-h.sum()` each iteration. Fine.

In micro adjust positive, if choose idx with max room but all at1, break. target<=n. Good.

`grad_active`:
```
def grad_active(h,n,active,weights):
   grad=np.zeros(n,float)

def slsqp_weighted_phase(h,n,deadline,T=8,maxiter=15):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_exact(h,n)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slsqp_eq,'jac':_slsqp_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   for cyc in range(2):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      q=compute_q(h,n); active,weights=select_active(q,n,T)
      try res=minimize(_slsqp_obj,h,args=(n,active,weights),jac=_slsqp_grad,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      except: continue
      h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=c5_exact(h_new,n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-14: best_h,h? set h=h_new; best_h=h_new; best_c=c
      else: h=best_h? keep best
   return best_h,best_c
```

```
def build_binary_lists(h,n):
    ones=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(h)]
    zeros=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(1.0-h)]
    one_pos={int(i):p for p,i in enumerate(ones)}
    zero_pos={int(i):p for p,i in enumerate(zeros)}
    return ones,zeros,one_pos,zero_pos
```
Dictionary comprehension uses no lambda? It is okay, not closure/lambda? Comprehension technically creates function? The rule says no closures or lambdas; dict comprehension may be okay but to be safe use explicit loops.
```
def build_binary_lists(h,n):
   ones=[]; zeros=[]; one_pos={}; zero_pos={}
   for i in range(n):
      hi=float(h[i])
      if hi > 0.5:
         one_pos[int(i)]=len(ones); ones.append(int(i))
      else:
         zero_pos[int(i)]=len(zeros); zeros.append(int(i))
   return ones,zeros,one_pos,zero_pos
```
Good.

Pseudo pair_polish code with loops and breaks needs careful. Let's write in final carefully.

```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    q = compute_q(h,n)
    dx = 2.0/n
    cur_count = float(np.max(q))
    best_count = cur_count
    best_h = h.copy()
    center = n-1
    B = 12 if n <= 400 else 8 if n <= 800 else 6
    T = 12 if n <= 400 else 16
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    tol = 1e-9
    for scale in PAIR_SCALES:
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0: break
        improved_in_scale = True
        while improved_in_scale and time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
            q = compute_q(h,n)
            cur_count = float(np.max(q))
            if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                best_count = cur_count; best_h = h.copy()
            elif cur_count > best_count + tol:
                h = best_h.copy(); q = compute_q(h,n); cur_count = best_count
                break
            active, weights = select_active(q,n,T)
            g = grad_active(h,n,active,weights)
            order = np.argsort(g)
            low = order[:B]
            high = order[-B:][::-1]
            improved = False
            moves = 0
            # guided pairs
            for ii in range(B):
                if moves >= 80: break
                i = int(low[ii])
                for jj in range(B):
                    if moves >= 80: break
                    j = int(high[jj])
                    if i == j: continue
                    res = try_guided_pair(h,q,n,i,j,center,g[i],g[j],scale,cur_count,tol)
                    if res is not None:
                        delta, new_count, z = res? But choose_delta returns delta,new_count; we need z for q update. Could compute z outside. Avoid repeated code.
```

```
def attempt_pair(h,q,n,i,j,center,pref,scale,target_count,tol):
    hi = float(h[i]); hj = float(h[j])
    Ld = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    Ud = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
    if Ud - Ld <= 1e-13: return None
    pref = 1.0 if pref >= 0 else -1.0
    z = make_pair_z(h,n,i,j)
    deltas = make_deltas(Ld,Ud,pref,scale)
    if not deltas: return None
    c1 = center + (j - i)
    c2 = center + (i - j)
    res = choose_delta(q,z,center,c1,c2,deltas,target_count,tol)
    if res is None: return None
    delta, new_count = res
    h[i] += delta
    h[j] -= delta
    d2 = delta*delta
    q += delta*z
    q[c1] += d2
    q[c2] += d2
    q[center] -= 2.0*d2
    return delta, new_count
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

BASE_STEPS = (
    0.05, 0.03, 0.02, 0.015, 0.01, 0.007, 0.005, 0.0035,
    0.0025, 0.0015, 0.001, 0.0007, 0.0005, 0.0003,
    0.0002, 0.00012, 0.00008, 0.00005, 0.00003, 0.00002
)

PAIR_SCALES = (
    1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125,
    0.015625, 0.0078125, 0.00390625, 0.001953125
)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(20):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if sub <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] -= sub

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def default_initial(n=300):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    rng = np.random.RandomState(987654321)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    idx = rng.choice(n, target, replace=False)
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def get_initial_h():
    try:
        return np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        return default_initial(300)


def c5_exact(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def compute_q(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    q = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    q[n - 1] = float(np.dot(h, omh))
    for k in range(1, n):
        nv = n - k
        q[n - 1 + k] = float(np.dot(h[:nv], omh[k:]))
        q[n - 1 - k] = float(np.dot(h[k:], omh[:nv]))
    return q


def c5_from_q(q, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(q)) * dx


def resolve_to(h, n):
    if len(h) == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h1 = np.interp(x1, x0, h, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return project_feasible(h1, n)


def symmetrize(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    hs = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(hs, len(hs))


def make_binary(h, n):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    if len(h) != n:
        h = resolve_to(h, n)
    target = n // 2
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[-target:]
        hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb


def select_active(q, n, T, beta=8.0):
    L = len(q)
    T = max(1, min(int(T), L))
    if T >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -T)[-T:]

    vals = q[idx].astype(float)
    order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    idx = idx[order]
    vals = vals[order]

    rng = float(vals[0] - vals[-1])
    if rng < 1e-10:
        w = np.full(T, 1.0 / T, dtype=float)
    else:
        w = np.exp(-beta * (vals - vals[0]) / max(rng, 1e-12))
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
            w = np.full(T, 1.0 / T, dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / sw
    return idx, w


def add_lag_grad(h, n, grad, idx, wt):
    k = idx - (n - 1)
    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        grad[:nv] += wt * (1.0 - h[k:])
        grad[k:] += wt * (-h[:nv])
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        grad[kk:] += wt * (1.0 - h[:nv])
        grad[:nv] += wt * (-h[kk:])


def weighted_obj(x, n, active, weights, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    val = 0.0
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        if wt <= 1e-14:
            continue
        k = idx - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            val += wt * float(np.dot(x[:nv], 1.0 - x[k:]))
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            val += wt * float(np.dot(x[kk:], 1.0 - x[:nv]))
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    return float(dx * val + mu * diff * diff)


def weighted_grad(x, n, active, weights, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        if wt > 1e-14:
            add_lag_grad(x, n, grad, idx, wt)
    grad *= dx
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
        if diff != 0.0:
            grad += 2.0 * mu * diff
    return grad


def active_lbfgs_phase(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    q = compute_q(h, n)
    best_c = c5_from_q(q, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 400:
        base_iter = 70
    elif n <= 800:
        base_iter = 42
    else:
        base_iter = 26

    configs = [
        (6, 10.0, base_iter),
        (12, 100.0, int(0.8 * base_iter)),
        (24, 100.0, int(0.6 * base_iter)),
    ]

    for T, mu, maxit in configs:
        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
            q = compute_q(h, n)
            active, weights = select_active(q, n, T)
            opt_kwargs = {
                "method": "L-BFGS-B",
                "bounds": bounds,
                "options": {
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxls": 50
                }
            }
            try:
                res = minimize(weighted_obj, h, args=(n, active, weights, mu),
                               jac=weighted_grad, **opt_kwargs)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x"):
                continue
            rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
                continue
            h_new = project_feasible(rx, n)
            c_new = c5_exact(h_new, n)
            if np.isfinite(c_new) and c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = float(c_new)
                best_h = h_new.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def _slsqp_eq_fun(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)


def _slsqp_obj(x, n, active, weights):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    val = 0.0
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        if wt <= 1e-14:
            continue
        k = idx - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            val += wt * float(np.dot(x[:nv], 1.0 - x[k:]))
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            val += wt * float(np.dot(x[kk:], 1.0 - x[:nv]))
    return float(dx * val)


def _slsqp_grad(x, n, active, weights):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        if wt > 1e-14:
            add_lag_grad(x, n, grad, idx, wt)
    grad *= dx
    return grad


def slsqp_weighted_phase(h, n, deadline, T=8, maxiter=15):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_exact(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = ({"type": "eq", "fun": _slsqp_eq_fun, "args": (n,)},)

    for _ in range(2):
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        q = compute_q(h, n)
        active, weights = select_active(q, n, T)
        try:
            res = minimize(_slsqp_obj, h, args=(n, active, weights),
                           jac=_slsqp_grad, method="SLSQP", bounds=bounds,
                           constraints=cons,
                           options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12})
        except Exception:
            continue
        if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x"):
            continue
        rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
            continue
        h_new = project_feasible(rx, n)
        c_new = c5_exact(h_new, n)
        if np.isfinite(c_new) and c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = float(c_new)
            best_h = h_new.copy()
            h = h_new

    return best_h, best_c


def make_pair_z(h, n, i, j):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    z = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    mhr = -h[::-1]
    s1 = n - 1 - i
    s2 = n - 1 - j
    z[s1:s1 + n] += omh
    z[s2:s2 + n] -= omh
    z[i:i + n] += mhr
    z[j:j + n] -= mhr
    return z


def make_deltas(ld, ud, pref, scale):
    span = ud - ld
    if span <= 1e-14:
        return []
    pref = 1.0 if pref >= 0.0 else -1.0
    vals = []
    maxc = 24

    for s in BASE_STEPS:
        d = pref * s * scale
        if d > ld and d < ud:
            vals.append(float(d))
            if len(vals) >= maxc:
                break
        d = -pref * s * scale
        if d > ld and d < ud and len(vals) < maxc:
            vals.append(float(d))

    mid = 0.5 * (ld + ud)
    for f in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7):
        if len(vals) >= maxc:
            break
        for sgn in (pref, -pref):
            d = mid + sgn * f * 0.5 * span
            if d > ld and d < ud:
                vals.append(float(d))
                if len(vals) >= maxc:
                    break

    uniq = []
    seen = set()
    for v in vals:
        if v <= ld or v >= ud:
            continue
        key = round(float(v), 14)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            uniq.append(float(v))
    return uniq


def choose_delta(q, z, center, c1, c2, deltas, target_count, tol=1e-9):
    d = np.asarray(deltas, dtype=float)
    if d.size == 0:
        return None
    d2 = d * d
    tmp = q[None, :] + d[:, None] * z[None, :]
    tmp[:, c1] += d2
    tmp[:, c2] += d2
    tmp[:, center] -= 2.0 * d2
    vals = tmp.max(axis=1)
    imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if vals[imin] < target_count - tol:
        return float(d[imin]), float(vals[imin])
    return None


def attempt_pair(h, q, n, i, j, center, pref, scale, target_count, tol):
    hi = float(h[i])
    hj = float(h[j])
    ld = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    ud = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
    if ud - ld <= 1e-13:
        return None

    z = make_pair_z(h, n, i, j)
    deltas = make_deltas(ld, ud, pref, scale)
    if not deltas:
        return None

    c1 = center + (j - i)
    c2 = center + (i - j)
    res = choose_delta(q, z, center, c1, c2, deltas, target_count, tol)
    if res is None:
        return None

    delta, _ = res
    h[i] += delta
    h[j] -= delta
    d2 = delta * delta
    q += delta * z
    q[c1] += d2
    q[c2] += d2
    q[center] -= 2.0 * d2
    return delta


def grad_active(h, n, active, weights):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        if wt > 1e-14:
            add_lag_grad(h, n, grad, idx, wt)
    return grad


def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    q = compute_q(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cur_count = float(np.max(q))
    best_count = cur_count
    best_h = h.copy()
    center = n - 1
    tol = 1e-9

    if n <= 400:
        B = 12
        T = 12
    elif n <= 800:
        B = 8
        T = 14
    else:
        B = 6
        T = 16

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    for scale in PAIR_SCALES:
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        improved_in_scale = True

        while improved_in_scale and time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
            q = compute_q(h, n)
            cur_count = float(np.max(q))
            if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                best_count = cur_count
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif cur_count > best_count + tol:
                h = best_h.copy()
                q = compute_q(h, n)
                cur_count = best_count
                break

            active, weights = select_active(q, n, T)
            g = grad_active(h, n, active, weights)
            order = np.argsort(g)
            low = order[:B]
            high = order[-B:][::-1]

            improved = False
            moves = 0

            for ii in range(B):
                if moves >= 70:
                    break
                i = int(low[ii])
                for jj in range(B):
                    if moves >= 70:
                        break
                    j = int(high[jj])
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    pref = 1.0 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1.0
                    res = attempt_pair(h, q, n, i, j, center, pref, scale, cur_count, tol)
                    if res is not None:
                        cur_count = float(np.max(q))
                        moves += 1
                        improved = True
                        if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                            best_count = cur_count
                            best_h = h.copy()

            if time.time() < deadline - 1.0 and moves < 120:
                rand_count = min(24, max(6, B // 2))
                for _ in range(rand_count):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        break
                    i = int(rng.randrange(n))
                    j = int(rng.randrange(n))
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    pref = 1.0 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1.0
                    res = attempt_pair(h, q, n, i, j, center, pref, scale, cur_count, tol)
                    if res is not None:
                        cur_count = float(np.max(q))
                        improved = True
                        if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                            best_count = cur_count
                            best_h = h.copy()

            if not improved:
                improved_in_scale = False
            else:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                q = compute_q(h, n)
                cur_count = float(np.max(q))
                if cur_count < best_count - tol:
                    best_count = cur_count
                    best_h = h.copy()
                elif cur_count > best_count + 1e-7:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    q = compute_q(h, n)
                    cur_count = best_count
                    improved_in_scale = False

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    q = compute_q(h, n)
    cur_count = float(np.max(q))
    if cur_count < best_count:
        best_count = cur_count
        best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_count * dx


def build_binary_lists(h, n):
    ones = []
    zeros = []
    one_pos = {}
    zero_pos = {}
    for i in range(n):
        hi = float(h[i])
        if hi > 0.5:
            p = len(ones)
            ones.append(int(i))
            one_pos[int(i)] = p
        else:
            p = len(zeros)
            zeros.append(int(i))
            zero_pos[int(i)] = p
    return ones, zeros, one_pos, zero_pos


def binary_anneal(h, n, deadline, seed=0):
    h = make_binary(h, n)
    n = len(h)
    q = compute_q(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cur_count = float(np.max(q))
    best_count = cur_count
    best_h = h.copy()
    center = n - 1

    ones, zeros, one_pos, zero_pos = build_binary_lists(h, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    temps = (2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625)
    for temp in temps:
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
            break

        if cur_count > best_count + max(1.0, 0.5 * temp):
            h = best_h.copy()
            q = compute_q(h, n)
            cur_count = best_count
            ones, zeros, one_pos, zero_pos = build_binary_lists(h, n)

        no_acc = 0
        max_no_acc = 20000 if temp >= 0.9 else 6000

        while time.time() < deadline - 1.0 and no_acc < max_no_acc:
            if not ones or not zeros:
                break

            i = zeros[int(rng.randrange(len(zeros)))]
            j = ones[int(rng.randrange(len(ones)))]

            z = make_pair_z(h, n, i, j)
            c1 = center + (j - i)
            c2 = center + (i - j)

            tmp = q + z
            tmp[c1] += 1.0
            tmp[c2] += 1.0
            tmp[center] -= 2.0
            new_count = float(np.max(tmp))

            if new_count <= cur_count + temp:
                h[i] = 1.0
                h[j] = 0.0
                q = tmp
                cur_count = new_count

                if cur_count < best_count:
                    best_count = cur_count
                    best_h = h.copy()

                p = zero_pos[i]
                last = zeros[-1]
                zeros[p] = last
                zero_pos[last] = p
                zeros.pop()
                del zero_pos[i]

                p = len(ones)
                ones.append(i)
                one_pos[i] = p

                p = one_pos[j]
                last = ones[-1]
                ones[p] = last
                one_pos[last] = p
                ones.pop()
                del one_pos[j]

                p = len(zeros)
                zeros.append(j)
                zero_pos[j] = p

                no_acc = 0
            else:
                no_acc += 1

        if cur_count > best_count + 0.8:
            h = best_h.copy()
            q = compute_q(h, n)
            cur_count = best_count
            ones, zeros, one_pos, zero_pos = build_binary_lists(h, n)

    return best_h, best_count * dx, n


def run_candidate(h, n, end, active_s, seed, use_binary=False):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)

    if use_binary and time.time() < end - 30.0:
        bin_end = min(end - 60.0, time.time() + max(30.0, min(90.0, float(active_s))))
        if bin_end > time.time() + 10.0:
            h, _, n = binary_anneal(h, n, bin_end, seed)

    if time.time() < end - 30.0 and active_s > 0:
        act_end = min(end - 30.0, time.time() + float(active_s))
        if act_end > time.time() + 5.0:
            h, _ = active_lbfgs_phase(h, n, act_end)

    if n <= 500 and time.time() < end - 25.0:
        h, _ = slsqp_weighted_phase(h, n, min(end - 10.0, time.time() + 25.0), T=8, maxiter=15)

    if time.time() < end - 2.0:
        h, _ = pair_polish(h, n, end - 1.0, seed + 1000)

    return h, c5_exact(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    deadline = t0 + total

    h_init = get_initial_h()
    n0 = len(h_init)
    h0 = project_feasible(h_init, n0)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c5_exact(best_h, best_n)

    cand_list = []

    hs = symmetrize(h0)
    cs = c5_exact(hs, len(hs))
    if np.isfinite(cs) and not np.allclose(hs, h0, atol=1e-12, rtol=1e-12):
        cand_list.append((hs, len(hs), float(cs)))
    if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c:
        best_h = hs.copy()
        best_n = len(hs)
        best_c = float(cs)

    n2 = max(2 * n0, 600)
    if n2 > 1500:
        n2 = n0
    if n2 % 2 != 0:
        n2 += 1

    if n2 > n0:
        h2 = resolve_to(h0, n2)
        c2 = c5_exact(h2, n2)
        if np.isfinite(c2):
            cand_list.append((h2, n2, float(c2)))
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_n = n2
                best_c = float(c2)

        h2s = symmetrize(h2)
        c2s = c5_exact(h2s, n2)
        if np.isfinite(c2s) and not np.allclose(h2s, h2, atol=1e-12, rtol=1e-12):
            cand_list.append((h2s, n2, float(c2s)))
            if c2s < best_c:
                best_h = h2s.copy()
                best_n = n2
                best_c = float(c2s)

    reserve = min(220.0, 0.3 * total)
    p1 = t0 + max(90.0, min(total * 0.45, 560.0))
    p1 = min(p1, deadline - reserve)
    if p1 < t0 + 45.0:
        p1 = t0 + 45.0

    if time.time() < p1 - 20.0:
        act1 = max(0.0, min(105.0, p1 - time.time() - 80.0))
        hc, cc = run_candidate(best_h, best_n, p1, act1, int(seed), False)
        if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
            best_h = np.asarray(hc, dtype=float).copy()
            best_c = float(cc)
            best_n = len(best_h)

    p2 = t0 + min(total * 0.78, 950.0)
    p2 = min(p2, deadline - 85.0)

    if cand_list and time.time() < p2 - 45.0:
        chosen = None
        chosen_c = float("inf")
        for item in cand_list:
            if item[2] < chosen_c:
                chosen_c = item[2]
                chosen = item
        if chosen is not None:
            act2 = max(0.0, min(70.0, p2 - time.time() - 55.0))
            hc, cc = run_candidate(chosen[0], chosen[1], p2, act2, int(seed) + 1, False)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_h = np.asarray(hc, dtype=float).copy()
                best_c = float(cc)
                best_n = len(best_h)

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        nb = best_n if best_n % 2 == 0 else best_n + 1
        if nb > 600:
            nb = 300
        hbin = make_binary(best_h, nb)
        end3 = min(deadline - 70.0, time.time() + min(120.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.time() - 70.0)))
        if end3 > time.time() + 30.0:
            hc, cc = run_candidate(hbin, nb, end3, 25.0, int(seed) + 2, True)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_h = np.asarray(hc, dtype=float).copy()
                best_c = float(cc)
                best_n = len(best_h)

    if time.time() < deadline - 3.0:
        hf, cf = pair_polish(best_h, best_n, deadline - 1.0, int(seed) + 99)
        if np.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
            best_h = np.asarray(hf, dtype=float).copy()
            best_c = float(cf)
            best_n = len(best_h)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_exact(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
